sequence.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>Many valid sequences share a small set of important constraints. The consequential failure is Individually reasonable rules conflict, disable all useful actions or permit apparent completion with pending obligations.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>Individually reasonable rules conflict, disable all useful actions or permit apparent completion with pending obligations.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Express necessary temporal/data constraints, analyse their consistency and progress implications, and explain which obligations remain pending before completion.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>Many valid sequences share a small set of important constraints.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>Use an imperative route for stable routine sections or a short set of human-understood rules when a declarative engine adds no value.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/cb.md#r-cba92e844a34bcc34b69beb53a427238cef445d2d577b7dbc8ccc1cce282f98f">Complete shared DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS · cba92e844a34</a></p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/77.md#r-77288c68ab38d7c8c1f2290f8010260b4473f98d4bd06c2cf294651ee0f12832">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · 77288c68ab38</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>Avoid or expose the controlled failure: Individually reasonable rules conflict, disable all useful actions or permit apparent completion with pending obligations.</p></dd><dt>Evidence qualification</dt><dd><p>A mechanism-level rationale, not a measured universal benefit. Adaptive, declarative and case approaches have distinct formal/design support. Comparative human and long-running deployment evidence is limited; flexibility is not one universal mechanism.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Constraint author, case worker and verification consumer.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>An allowed trace is not automatically authorised or wise; external powers remain separately supplied.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Expose why an action is allowed/blocked and which obligations remain to be fulfilled.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>The rule set admits the intended cases and rejects specified violations, including terminal response obligations.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Individually reasonable rules conflict, disable all useful actions or permit apparent completion with pending obligations.</p></li><li><p>Constraint interactions can be hard to explain; permissiveness and complexity may overwhelm users.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/fb.md#r-fbcc48701b36c59041a5e08feb1098aeb5f11a3b5e3aa2d1565b5711e0e99d08">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · fbcc48701b36</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>HYBRIDISED: constraint-based analysis supports flexibility, while routine imperative fragments remain legitimate alternatives.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Express necessary temporal/data constraints, analyse their consistency and progress implications, and explain which obligations remain pending before completion. required and repetition rules, and explicit lifecycle completion conditions.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>Case workers add or activate work during a case. The consequential failure is Discretionary work becomes silently mandatory, or failed required work is misreported as successful because a stage closed.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>Discretionary work becomes silently mandatory, or failed required work is misreported as successful because a stage closed.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Separate the available plan from selected work, declare who may add/activate items, and define completion without equating every terminal item state with success.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>Case workers add or activate work during a case.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>A small case plan with explicit decisions can replace a full case-management notation.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/16.md#r-16f94818ac349ea86fd33acce3705fd52b9edab1b685e526732bf2320518dfaf">Complete shared DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS · 16f94818ac34</a></p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/56.md#r-56ff5f24cf9a914fde897c99c1f399e59b259d49f4783461110d3e26863269ac">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · 56ff5f24cf9a</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>Avoid or expose the controlled failure: Discretionary work becomes silently mandatory, or failed required work is misreported as successful because a stage closed.</p></dd><dt>Evidence qualification</dt><dd><p>A mechanism-level rationale, not a measured universal benefit. Adaptive, declarative and case approaches have distinct formal/design support. Comparative human and long-running deployment evidence is limited; flexibility is not one universal mechanism.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Case planner, worker and completion consumer.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>Planning tables express permitted roles in a model; they do not establish external authority.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Communicate what was available, chosen, declined, completed or otherwise terminated.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>A closed case states its actual outcome and selected-work disposition, not merely its engine lifecycle label.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Discretionary work becomes silently mandatory, or failed required work is misreported as successful because a stage closed.</p></li><li><p>Planning complexity, hidden obligations and over-reading of “required” flags.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><dl><dt>Adverse mechanism or cost</dt><dd><p>Planning complexity, hidden obligations and over-reading of “required” flags.</p></dd><dt>Criticism ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-C009</p></li><li><p>EWM-C017</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Tension ids</dt><dd><ol></ol></dd><dt>Critical source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S016</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Adjudication</dt><dd><p>Conditional case-management mechanism; no compulsory CMMN adoption.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>REFINED: case completion is treated according to its explicit standard semantics and separately evaluated business meaning.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Separate the available plan from selected work, declare who may add/activate items, and define completion without equating every terminal item state with success. constrain an overclaim rather than create an obligation?</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Native record</dt><dd><dl><dt>Property id</dt><dd><p>EWM-056</p></dd><dt>Property name</dt><dd><p>Conformance observations as bounded evidence</p></dd><dt>Lineage id</dt><dd><p>EVOLVED_WORKFLOW_MANAGEMENT</p></dd><dt>Candidate origin</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/12.md#r-120b9d3c2d03b0e63b351859394679c6bc5aa5f5b71a682bde4259306ab7180d">Complete shared CANDIDATE ORIGIN · 120b9d3c2d03</a></p></dd><dt>Historical origin</dt><dd><dl><dt>Formulation</dt><dd><p>Conformance methods compare observed event traces with model behaviour using measures such as fitness and appropriateness.</p></dd><dt>Genealogy node ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-G17</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Source ids</dt><dd><ol><li><p>EWM-S043</p></li><li><p>EWM-S045</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Limit</dt><dd><p>Dates and exact inspected editions resolve in SOURCE_TABLE; this is not a claim of earliest invention.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Lineage class</dt><dd><p>NATIVE_WITH_SHARED_ANCESTRY_AND_DOCUMENTED_INTERSECTIONS</p></dd><dt>Original form</dt><dd><p>Conformance methods compare observed event traces with model behaviour using measures such as fitness and appropriateness.</p></dd><dt>Problem it addressed</dt><dd><p>A workflow is being monitored or assessed against an expected model. The consequential failure is Unlogged work appears absent, recording errors appear as misconduct, or high fitness is mistaken for a good process.</p></dd><dt>Failure mode it tries to prevent</dt><dd><p>Unlogged work appears absent, recording errors appear as misconduct, or high fitness is mistaken for a good process.</p></dd><dt>Mechanism</dt><dd><p>Check log-to-case/activity/order assumptions and the model’s intended scope before interpreting deviations or conformance as evidence about enactment.</p></dd><dt>Trigger or context</dt><dd><p>A workflow is being monitored or assessed against an expected model.</p></dd><dt>Non trigger or cheap path</dt><dd><p>Directly review a small set of consequential traces instead of imposing a general mining pipeline.</p></dd><dt>Dependencies or preconditions</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/7b.md#r-7b2f9d2629ccd3743671bc4e6068150f5fa42d946b3610917b94af7b53e6c3be">Complete shared DEPENDENCIES OR PRECONDITIONS · 7b2f9d2629cc</a></p></dd><dt>Domain profile</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/f2.md#r-f21284d52e7b468810243ae16dc8354a2cffea82edd5906052c1e69e6f108494">Complete shared DOMAIN PROFILE · f21284d52e7b</a></p></dd><dt>Expected engineering payoff</dt><dd><dl><dt>Claim</dt><dd><p>Avoid or expose the controlled failure: Unlogged work appears absent, recording errors appear as misconduct, or high fitness is mistaken for a good process.</p></dd><dt>Evidence qualification</dt><dd><p>A mechanism-level rationale, not a measured universal benefit. The evidence mixes an older longitudinal programme, recent scientific systems/operational observations and new AI/low-code audits and controlled prototype studies. They are neither equivalent endpoints nor independent replications of one proposition.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Operational reviewer and participants asked to explain deviations.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>A detected mismatch is a question for adjudication, not automatic authority to sanction or repair.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Present the observed trace, alignment assumptions and plausible recording/model explanations.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>Conformance claims state what was observed and distinguish model, logging and real-work deviations.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Unlogged work appears absent, recording errors appear as misconduct, or high fitness is mistaken for a good process.</p></li><li><p>Metric gaming, model overfit and loss of many-object interactions in a single-case projection.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/e2.md#r-e2b65cda9483da5954d7cb91c04fa5825b5882878e97b4527d59903a5da76db3">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · e2b65cda9483</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>REFINED: conformance remains evidence for inquiry, not proof of total correctness or external success.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Check log-to-case/activity/order assumptions and the model’s intended scope before interpreting deviations or conformance as evidence about enactment. Assumption-sensitive interface with process mining; no sibling synthesis was consulted.</p></dd><dt>Ceremony vs property</dt><dd><dl><dt>Protected function</dt><dd><p>Unlogged work appears absent, recording errors appear as misconduct, or high fitness is mistaken for a good process.</p></dd><dt>Minimal adequate alternative</dt><dd><p>Directly review a small set of consequential traces instead of imposing a general mining pipeline.</p></dd><dt>Fuller form trigger</dt><dd><p>A workflow is being monitored or assessed against an expected model.</p></dd><dt>Consumer</dt><dd><p>Operational reviewer and participants asked to explain deviations.</p></dd><dt>Cost of simplification</dt><dd><p>Removing the mechanism is acceptable only while the cheap path still establishes this claim: Conformance claims state what was observed and distinguish model, logging and real-work deviations.</p></dd><dt>Retirement or omission</dt><dd><p>Retire or omit the extra machinery when the trigger, consumer or relevant dependency disappears, after settling/transferring the outstanding work or evidence that still relies on it. The evidence mixes an older longitudinal programme, recent scientific systems/operational observations and new AI/low-code audits and controlled prototype studies. They are neither equivalent endpoints nor independent replications of one proposition.</p></dd></dl></dd><dt>Decision or consumer</dt><dd><p>Operational coordinator, affected workers and service recipients.</p></dd><dt>Actor and authority boundary</dt><dd><p>Monitoring does not authorise intervention beyond the responsible actor’s powers.</p></dd><dt>Communication or interaction requirement</dt><dd><p>Communicate the actionable condition and competing explanations rather than only a red/green indicator.</p></dd><dt>Observable postcondition</dt><dd><p>A material signal results in an appropriate decision, including justified non-intervention.</p></dd><dt>Known failure modes</dt><dd><ol><li><p>Dashboards accumulate while no one can act, or people optimise status metrics instead of results.</p></li><li><p>Surveillance, alert fatigue, gaming and over-prioritisation of frequent but low-cost errors.</p></li></ol></dd><dt>Important criticisms</dt><dd><p><a href="../../data/relationships/shared/77.md#r-776b542cc2f70a3450c26cfb92b3a3d7bf025cc120c77bd8fadad16f16b153de">Complete shared IMPORTANT CRITICISMS · 776b542cc2f7</a></p></dd><dt>How the property evolved</dt><dd><p>REFINED: monitoring is retained as a feedback mechanism with a consumer, not a display requirement.</p></dd><dt>Mature or evolved form</dt><dd><p>Connect each monitored quantity to a decision-maker and a possible action, while preserving scope, uncertainty and incentives.
